     EN: They turned back and came to the spring of Mishpat, which is Kadesh, and struck the whole region of the Amalekites and the Amorite who lived in Hazazon-tamar.

14:8  LA: Et egressi sunt rex Sodomorum, et rex Gomorrhæ, rexque Adamæ, et rex Seboim, necnon et rex Balæ, quæ est Segor : et direxerunt aciem contra eos in valle Silvestri :
     EN: The king of Sodom, the king of Gomorrah, the king of Admah, the king of Zeboiim, and the king of Bela, which is Zoar, went out and drew up their battle line against them in the Wooded Valley,

14:9  LA: scilicet adversus Chodorlahomor regem Elamitarum, et Thadal regem Gentium, et Amraphel regem Sennaar, et Arioch regem Ponti : quatuor reges adversus quinque.
     EN: against Chedorlaomer king of the Elamites, Tidal king of the nations, Amraphel king of Shinar, and Arioch king of Pontus: four kings against five.

14:10  LA: Vallis autem Silvestris habebat puteos multos bituminis. Itaque rex Sodomorum, et Gomorrhæ, terga verterunt, cecideruntque ibi : et qui remanserant, fugerunt ad montem.
     EN: The Wooded Valley had many bitumen pits. The kings of Sodom and Gomorrah turned in flight and fell there, while those who remained fled to the mountain.

14:11  LA: Tulerunt autem omnem substantiam Sodomorum et Gomorrhæ, et universa quæ ad cibum pertinent, et abierunt :
     EN: The victors took all the possessions of Sodom and Gomorrah and all their food supplies, and went away.

14:12  LA: necnon et Lot, et substantiam ejus, filium fratris Abram, qui habitabat in Sodomis.
     EN: They also took Lot, Abram's brother's son, who lived in Sodom, and his possessions.

14:13  LA: Et ecce unus, qui evaserat, nuntiavit Abram Hebræo, qui habitabat in convalle Mambre Amorrhæi, fratris Escol, et fratris Aner : hi enim pepigerant fœdus cum Abram.
     EN: One who escaped reported this to Abram the Hebrew, who lived in the valley of Mamre the Amorite, brother of Eshcol and Aner; for these had made a covenant with Abram.

14:14  LA: Quod cum audisset Abram, captum videlicet Lot fratrem suum, numeravit expeditos vernaculos suos trecentos decem et octo : et persecutus est usque Dan.
     EN: When Abram heard that his kinsman Lot had been captured, he mustered his three hundred eighteen armed men born in his household and pursued as far as Dan.

14:15  LA: Et divisis sociis, irruit super eos nocte : percussitque eos, et persecutus est eos usque Hoba, quæ est ad lævam Damasci.
     EN: He divided his companions, fell upon the enemy by night, struck them, and pursued them as far as Hobah, which is to the left of Damascus.

14:16  LA: Reduxitque omnem substantiam, et Lot fratrem suum cum substantia illius, mulieres quoque et populum.
     EN: He brought back all the possessions, his kinsman Lot with his possessions, and also the women and the people.

14:17  LA: Egressus est autem rex Sodomorum in occursum ejus postquam reversus est a cæde Chodorlahomor, et regum qui cum eo erant in valle Save, quæ est vallis regis.
     EN: After he returned from defeating Chedorlaomer and the kings with him, the king of Sodom went out to meet him in the valley of Shaveh, which is the King's Valley.

14:18  LA: At vero Melchisedech rex Salem, proferens panem et vinum, erat enim sacerdos Dei altissimi,
     EN: Melchizedek king of Salem brought out bread and wine, for he was priest of God Most High,

14:19  LA: benedixit ei, et ait : Benedictus Abram Deo excelso, qui creavit cælum et terram :
     EN: and blessed him, saying, “Blessed be Abram by God Most High, who created heaven and earth;

14:20  LA: et benedictus Deus excelsus, quo protegente, hostes in manibus tuis sunt. Et dedit ei decimas ex omnibus.
     EN: and blessed be God Most High, by whose protection your enemies are in your hands.” And Abram gave him a tenth of everything.

14:21  LA: Dixit autem rex Sodomorum ad Abram : Da mihi animas, cetera tolle tibi.
     EN: The king of Sodom said to Abram, “Give me the people; take the rest for yourself.”

14:22  LA: Qui respondit ei : Levo manum meam ad Dominum Deum excelsum possessorem cæli et terræ,
     EN: He answered him, “I lift my hand to the Lord God Most High, possessor of heaven and earth,

14:23  LA: quod a filo subtegminis usque ad corigiam caligæ, non accipiam ex omnibus quæ tua sunt, ne dicas : Ego ditavi Abram :
     EN: that I will take nothing of yours, from a woven thread to a sandal strap, lest you say, ‘I made Abram rich.’

14:24  LA: exceptis his, quæ comederunt juvenes, et partibus virorum, qui venerunt mecum, Aner, Escol et Mambre : isti accipient partes suas.
     EN: Except what the young men have eaten, and the shares of the men who came with me—Aner, Eshcol, and Mamre. Let these take their shares.”

GENESIS 15

15:1  LA: His itaque transactis, factus est sermo Domini ad Abram per visionem dicens : Noli timere, Abram : ego protector tuus sum, et merces tua magna nimis.
     EN: After these things, the word of the Lord came to Abram in a vision, saying, “Do not fear, Abram. I am your protector and your exceedingly great reward.”

15:2  LA: Dixitque Abram : Domine Deus, quid dabis mihi ? ego vadam absque liberis, et filius procuratoris domus meæ iste Damascus Eliezer.
     EN: Abram said, “Lord God, what will You give me? I shall go without children, and the son of my household's steward is this Eliezer of Damascus.”

15:3  LA: Addiditque Abram : Mihi autem non dedisti semen, et ecce vernaculus meus, hæres meus erit.
     EN: Abram added, “You have given me no offspring, and see, one born in my household will be my heir.”

15:4  LA: Statimque sermo Domini factus est ad eum, dicens : Non erit hic hæres tuus, sed qui egredietur de utero tuo, ipsum habebis hæredem.
     EN: At once the word of the Lord came to him, saying, “This one shall not be your heir; rather, one who comes forth from your own body shall be your heir.”

15:5  LA: Eduxitque eum foras, et ait illi : Suspice cælum, et numera stellas, si potes. Et dixit ei : Sic erit semen tuum.
     EN: He brought him outside and said, “Look up at heaven and count the stars, if you can.” And He said to him, “So shall your offspring be.”

15:6  LA: Credidit Abram Deo, et reputatum est illi ad justitiam.
     EN: Abram believed God, and it was reckoned to him as righteousness.

15:7  LA: Dixitque ad eum : Ego Dominus qui eduxi te de Ur Chaldæorum ut darem tibi terram istam, et possideres eam.
     EN: He said to him, “I am the Lord who brought you out of Ur of the Chaldeans to give you this land to possess.”

15:8  LA: At ille ait : Domine Deus, unde scire possum quod possessurus sim eam ?
     EN: He said, “Lord God, how can I know that I shall possess it?”

15:9  LA: Et respondens Dominus : Sume, inquit, mihi vaccam triennem, et capram trimam, et arietem annorum trium, turturem quoque et columbam.
     EN: The Lord answered, “Take for Me a three-year-old cow, a three-year-old goat, a three-year-old ram, a turtledove, and a pigeon.”

15:10  LA: Qui tollens universa hæc, divisit ea per medium, et utrasque partes contra se altrinsecus posuit ; aves autem non divisit.
     EN: He took all these, divided them in the middle, and laid each half opposite the other; but he did not divide the birds.

15:11  LA: Descenderuntque volucres super cadavera, et abigebat eas Abram.
     EN: Birds descended upon the carcasses, and Abram drove them away.

15:12  LA: Cumque sol occumberet, sopor irruit super Abram, et horror magnus et tenebrosus invasit eum.
     EN: As the sun was setting, a deep sleep fell upon Abram, and a great, dark dread seized him.

15:13  LA: Dictumque est ad eum : Scito prænoscens quod peregrinum futurum sit semen tuum in terra non sua, et subjicient eos servituti, et affligent quadringentis annis.
     EN: He was told, “Know beforehand that your offspring shall be strangers in a land not their own. They shall subject them to slavery and afflict them for four hundred years.

15:14  LA: Verumtamen gentem, cui servituri sunt, ego judicabo : et post hæc egredientur cum magna substantia.
     EN: But I will judge the nation they serve, and afterward they shall come out with great possessions.

15:15  LA: Tu autem ibis ad patres tuos in pace, sepultus in senectute bona.
     EN: You shall go to your fathers in peace, buried in a good old age.

15:16  LA: Generatione autem quarta revertentur huc : necdum enim completæ sunt iniquitates Amorrhæorum usque ad præsens tempus.
     EN: In the fourth generation they shall return here, for even now the iniquities of the Amorites have not reached their full measure.”

15:17  LA: Cum ergo occubuisset sol, facta est caligo tenebrosa, et apparuit clibanus fumans, et lampas ignis transiens inter divisiones illas.
     EN: When the sun had set and deep darkness came, a smoking furnace appeared, and a flaming torch passed between those pieces.

15:18  LA: In illo die pepigit Dominus fœdus cum Abram, dicens : Semini tuo dabo terram hanc a fluvio Ægypti usque ad fluvium magnum Euphraten,
     EN: On that day the Lord made a covenant with Abram, saying, “To your offspring I will give this land, from the river of Egypt to the great river Euphrates:

15:19  LA: Cinæos, et Cenezæos, Cedmonæos,
     EN: the Kenites, the Kenizzites, the Kadmonites,

15:20  LA: et Hethæos, et Pherezæos, Raphaim quoque,
     EN: the Hittites, the Perizzites, the Rephaim also,

15:21  LA: et Amorrhæos, et Chananæos, et Gergesæos, et Jebusæos.
     EN: the Amorites, the Canaanites, the Girgashites, and the Jebusites.”

GENESIS 16

16:1  LA: Igitur Sarai, uxor Abram, non genuerat liberos : sed habens ancillam ægyptiam nomine Agar,
     EN: Sarai, Abram's wife, had borne no children, but she had an Egyptian maidservant named Hagar.

16:2  LA: dixit marito suo : Ecce, conclusit me Dominus, ne parerem. Ingredere ad ancillam meam, si forte saltem ex illa suscipiam filios. Cumque ille acquiesceret deprecanti,
     EN: She said to her husband, “See, the Lord has closed my womb so that I cannot bear children. Go in to my maidservant; perhaps through her at least I may receive children.” When he agreed to her request,

16:3  LA: tulit Agar ægyptiam ancillam suam post annos decem quam habitare cœperant in terra Chanaan : et dedit eam viro suo uxorem.
     EN: she took her Egyptian maidservant Hagar and gave her to her husband as a wife, ten years after they had begun living in the land of Canaan.

16:4  LA: Qui ingressus est ad eam. At illa concepisse se videns, despexit dominam suam.
     EN: He went in to her. When she saw that she had conceived, she despised her mistress.

16:5  LA: Dixitque Sarai ad Abram : Inique agis contra me : ego dedi ancillam meam in sinum tuum, quæ videns quod conceperit, despectui me habet : judicet Dominus inter me et te.
     EN: Sarai said to Abram, “You deal unjustly with me. I gave my maidservant into your embrace, and now that she sees she has conceived, she holds me in contempt. May the Lord judge between me and you.”

16:6  LA: Cui respondens Abram : Ecce, ait, ancilla tua in manu tua est, utere ea ut libet. Affligente igitur eam Sarai, fugam iniit.
     EN: Abram answered her, “See, your maidservant is in your hand; deal with her as you please.” So Sarai afflicted her, and she fled.

16:7  LA: Cumque invenisset eam angelus Domini juxta fontem aquæ in solitudine, qui est in via Sur in deserto,
     EN: The angel of the Lord found her beside a spring of water in the wilderness, on the road to Shur in the desert,

16:8  LA: dixit ad illam : Agar ancilla Sarai, unde venis ? et quo vadis ? Quæ respondit : A facie Sarai dominæ meæ ego fugio.
     EN: and said to her, “Hagar, Sarai's maidservant, where have you come from, and where are you going?” She answered, “I am fleeing from my mistress Sarai.”

16:9  LA: Dixitque ei angelus Domini : Revertere ad dominam tuam, et humiliare sub manu illius.
     EN: The angel of the Lord said to her, “Return to your mistress and humble yourself under her hand.”

16:10  LA: Et rursum : Multiplicans, inquit, multiplicabo semen tuum, et non numerabitur præ multitudine.
     EN: Again he said, “I will greatly multiply your offspring, and they shall be too many to be counted.”

16:11  LA: Ac deinceps : Ecce, ait, concepisti, et paries filium : vocabisque nomen ejus Ismaël, eo quod audierit Dominus afflictionem tuam.
     EN: Then he said, “See, you have conceived and shall bear a son. You shall name him Ishmael, because the Lord has heard your affliction.

16:12  LA: Hic erit ferus homo : manus ejus contra omnes, et manus omnium contra eum : et e regione universorum fratrum suorum figet tabernacula.
     EN: He shall be a wild man, his hand against everyone and everyone's hand against him; and he shall pitch his tents opposite all his brothers.”

16:13  LA: Vocavit autem nomen Domini qui loquebatur ad eam : Tu Deus qui vidisti me. Dixit enim : Profecto hic vidi posteriora videntis me.
     EN: She called the name of the Lord who spoke to her, “You are the God who has seen me.” For she said, “Surely here I have seen the back of Him who sees me.”

16:14  LA: Propterea appellavit puteum illum Puteum viventis et videntis me. Ipse est inter Cades et Barad.
     EN: Therefore she called that well the Well of the Living One Who Sees Me. It is between Kadesh and Bered.

16:15  LA: Peperitque Agar Abræ filium : qui vocavit nomen ejus Ismaël.
     EN: Hagar bore Abram a son, and he named him Ishmael.

16:16  LA: Octoginta et sex annorum erat Abram quando peperit ei Agar Ismaëlem.
     EN: Abram was eighty-six years old when Hagar bore him Ishmael.

GENESIS 17

17:1  LA: Postquam vero nonaginta et novem annorum esse cœperat, apparuit ei Dominus, dixitque ad eum : Ego Deus omnipotens : ambula coram me, et esto perfectus.
     EN: When he had reached ninety-nine years, the Lord appeared to him and said, “I am God Almighty. Walk before Me and be blameless.

17:2  LA: Ponamque fœdus meum inter me et te, et multiplicabo te vehementer nimis.
     EN: I will establish My covenant between Me and you, and multiply you exceedingly.”

17:3  LA: Cecidit Abram pronus in faciem.
     EN: Abram fell facedown.

17:4  LA: Dixitque ei Deus : Ego sum, et pactum meum tecum, erisque pater multarum gentium.
     EN: God said to him, “It is I, and My covenant is with you. You shall be the father of many nations.

17:5  LA: Nec ultra vocabitur nomen tuum Abram, sed appellaberis Abraham : quia patrem multarum gentium constitui te.
     EN: Your name shall no longer be Abram, but you shall be called Abraham, because I have appointed you the father of many nations.

17:6  LA: Faciamque te crescere vehementissime, et ponam te in gentibus, regesque ex te egredientur.
     EN: I will make you increase exceedingly, make nations of you, and kings shall come forth from you.

17:7  LA: Et statuam pactum meum inter me et te, et inter semen tuum post te in generationibus suis, fœdere sempiterno : ut sim Deus tuus, et seminis tui post te.
     EN: I will establish My covenant between Me and you and your offspring after you through their generations, as an everlasting covenant, to be your God and the God of your offspring after you.

17:8  LA: Daboque tibi et semini tuo terram peregrinationis tuæ, omnem terram Chanaan in possessionem æternam, eroque Deus eorum.
     EN: I will give you and your offspring the land of your sojourning, the whole land of Canaan, as an everlasting possession, and I will be their God.”

17:9  LA: Dixit iterum Deus ad Abraham : Et tu ergo custodies pactum meum, et semen tuum post te in generationibus suis.
     EN: God said again to Abraham, “You also shall keep My covenant, you and your offspring after you through their generations.

17:10  LA: Hoc est pactum meum quod observabitis inter me et vos, et semen tuum post te : circumcidetur ex vobis omne masculinum :
     EN: This is My covenant that you shall observe between Me and you and your offspring after you: every male among you shall be circumcised.

17:11  LA: et circumcidetis carnem præputii vestri, ut sit in signum fœderis inter me et vos.
     EN: You shall circumcise the flesh of your foreskin as a sign of the covenant between Me and you.

17:12  LA: Infans octo dierum circumcidetur in vobis, omne masculinum in generationibus vestris : tam vernaculus, quam emptitius circumcidetur, et quicumque non fuerit de stirpe vestra :
     EN: Every male infant among you shall be circumcised at eight days old throughout your generations. Both those born in your household and those purchased shall be circumcised, including whoever is not of your lineage.

17:13  LA: eritque pactum meum in carne vestra in fœdus æternum.
     EN: My covenant shall be in your flesh as an everlasting covenant.

17:14  LA: Masculus, cujus præputii caro circumcisa non fuerit, delebitur anima illa de populo suo : quia pactum meum irritum fecit.
     EN: A male whose foreskin's flesh has not been circumcised—that person shall be blotted out from his people, because he has broken My covenant.”

17:15  LA: Dixit quoque Deus ad Abraham : Sarai uxorem tuam non vocabis Sarai, sed Saram.
     EN: God also said to Abraham, “You shall not call your wife Sarai by the name Sarai, but Sarah.

17:16  LA: Et benedicam ei, et ex illa dabo tibi filium cui benedicturus sum : eritque in nationes, et reges populorum orientur ex eo.
     EN: I will bless her and give you a son by her, whom I will bless. He shall become nations, and kings of peoples shall arise from him.”

17:17  LA: Cecidit Abraham in faciem suam, et risit, dicens in corde suo : Putasne centenario nascetur filius ? et Sara nonagenaria pariet ?
     EN: Abraham fell upon his face and laughed, saying in his heart, “Can a son be born to a man of one hundred years? And shall Sarah, at ninety, give birth?”

17:18  LA: Dixitque ad Deum : Utinam Ismaël vivat coram te.
     EN: He said to God, “If only Ishmael might live before You!”

17:19  LA: Et ait Deus ad Abraham : Sara uxor tua pariet tibi filium, vocabisque nomen ejus Isaac, et constituam pactum meum illi in fœdus sempiternum, et semini ejus post eum.
     EN: God said to Abraham, “Sarah your wife shall bear you a son; you shall name him Isaac. I will establish My covenant with him as an everlasting covenant, and with his offspring after him.

17:20  LA: Super Ismaël quoque exaudivi te : ecce, benedicam ei, et augebo, et multiplicabo eum valde : duodecim duces generabit, et faciam illum in gentem magnam.
     EN: Concerning Ishmael also I have heard you. See, I will bless him, increase him, and multiply him greatly. He shall father twelve princes, and I will make him a great nation.

17:21  LA: Pactum vero meum statuam ad Isaac, quem pariet tibi Sara tempore isto in anno altero.
     EN: But My covenant I will establish with Isaac, whom Sarah shall bear you at this time next year.”

17:22  LA: Cumque finitus esset sermo loquentis cum eo, ascendit Deus ab Abraham.
     EN: When He had finished speaking with him, God went up from Abraham.

17:23  LA: Tulit autem Abraham Ismaël filium suum, et omnes vernaculos domus suæ, universosque quos emerat, cunctos mares ex omnibus viris domus suæ : et circumcidit carnem præputii eorum statim in ipsa die, sicut præceperat ei Deus.
     EN: Abraham took his son Ishmael, all those born in his house, and all those he had purchased—every male among the men of his household—and immediately, on that very day, circumcised the flesh of their foreskins, as God had commanded him.

17:24  LA: Abraham nonaginta et novem erat annorum quando circumcidit carnem præputii sui.
     EN: Abraham was ninety-nine years old when he circumcised the flesh of his foreskin.

17:25  LA: Et Ismaël filius tredecim annos impleverat tempore circumcisionis suæ.
     EN: His son Ishmael had reached thirteen years at the time of his circumcision.

17:26  LA: Eadem die circumcisus est Abraham et Ismaël filius ejus :
     EN: On that same day Abraham and his son Ishmael were circumcised,

17:27  LA: et omnes viri domus illius, tam vernaculi, quam emptitii et alienigenæ pariter circumcisi sunt.
     EN: and all the men of his house, those born there as well as those purchased and foreigners, were circumcised together.

GENESIS 18

18:1  LA: Apparuit autem ei Dominus in convalle Mambre sedenti in ostio tabernaculi sui in ipso fervore diei.
     EN: The Lord appeared to him in the valley of Mamre as he sat at his tent's entrance in the heat of the day.

18:2  LA: Cumque elevasset oculos, apparuerunt ei tres viri stantes prope eum : quos cum vidisset, cucurrit in occursum eorum de ostio tabernaculi, et adoravit in terram.
     EN: He raised his eyes, and three men appeared standing near him. When he saw them, he ran from the tent's entrance to meet them and bowed to the ground.

18:3  LA: Et dixit : Domine, si inveni gratiam in oculis tuis, ne transeas servum tuum :
     EN: He said, “Lord, if I have found favor in Your eyes, do not pass Your servant by.

18:4  LA: sed afferam pauxillum aquæ, et lavate pedes vestros, et requiescite sub arbore.
     EN: Let me bring a little water; wash your feet and rest beneath the tree.

18:5  LA: Ponamque buccellam panis, et confortate cor vestrum : postea transibitis : idcirco enim declinastis ad servum vestrum. Qui dixerunt : Fac ut locutus es.
     EN: I will set out a morsel of bread, and you may strengthen your hearts; afterward you may pass on, for this is why you have turned aside to your servant.” They said, “Do as you have said.”

18:6  LA: Festinavit Abraham in tabernaculum ad Saram, dixitque ei : Accelera, tria sata similæ commisce, et fac subcinericios panes.
     EN: Abraham hurried into the tent to Sarah and said to her, “Quickly, mix three seahs of fine flour and make loaves baked under the ashes.”

18:7  LA: Ipse vero ad armentum cucurrit, et tulit inde vitulum tenerrimum et optimum, deditque puero : qui festinavit et coxit illum.
     EN: He ran to the herd, took a very tender, choice calf, and gave it to a young servant, who hurried and cooked it.

18:8  LA: Tulit quoque butyrum et lac, et vitulum quem coxerat, et posuit coram eis : ipse vero stabat juxta eos sub arbore.
     EN: He also took butter and milk and the calf he had cooked, and set them before them, while he stood beside them beneath the tree.

18:9  LA: Cumque comedissent, dixerunt ad eum : Ubi est Sara uxor tua ? Ille respondit : Ecce in tabernaculo est.
     EN: When they had eaten, they said to him, “Where is Sarah your wife?” He answered, “There, in the tent.”

18:10  LA: Cui dixit : Revertens veniam ad te tempore isto, vita comite, et habebit filium Sara uxor tua. Quo audito, Sara risit post ostium tabernaculi.
     EN: He said to him, “I will return to you at this time, life continuing, and Sarah your wife shall have a son.” Hearing this, Sarah laughed behind the tent's entrance.

18:11  LA: Erant autem ambo senes, provectæque ætatis, et desierant Saræ fieri muliebria.
     EN: Both were old and advanced in age, and the ways of women had ceased for Sarah.

18:12  LA: Quæ risit occulte dicens : Postquam consenui, et dominus meus vetulus est, voluptati operam dabo ?
     EN: She laughed secretly, saying, “Now that I have grown old, and my lord is an old man, shall I give myself to pleasure?”

18:13  LA: Dixit autem Dominus ad Abraham : Quare risit Sara, dicens : Num vere paritura sum anus ?
     EN: The Lord said to Abraham, “Why did Sarah laugh, saying, ‘Shall I really give birth as an old woman?’

18:14  LA: Numquid Deo quidquam est difficile ? juxta condictum revertar ad te hoc eodem tempore, vita comite, et habebit Sara filium.
     EN: Is anything difficult for God? At the appointed time I will return to you, at this same time, life continuing, and Sarah shall have a son.”

18:15  LA: Negavit Sara, dicens : Non risi, timore perterrita. Dominus autem : Non est, inquit, ita : sed risisti.
     EN: Terrified, Sarah denied it, saying, “I did not laugh.” But the Lord said, “No; you did laugh.”

18:16  LA: Cum ergo surrexissent inde viri, direxerunt oculos contra Sodomam : et Abraham simul gradiebatur, deducens eos.
     EN: The men rose from there and looked toward Sodom, and Abraham walked with them to see them on their way.

18:17  LA: Dixitque Dominus : Num celare potero Abraham quæ gesturus sum :
     EN: The Lord said, “Can I conceal from Abraham what I am about to do,

18:18  LA: cum futurus sit in gentem magnam, ac robustissimam, et benedicendæ sint in illo omnes nationes terræ ?
     EN: when he is to become a great and very mighty nation, and all the nations of the earth are to be blessed in him?

18:19  LA: Scio enim quod præcepturus sit filiis suis, et domui suæ post se ut custodiant viam Domini, et faciant judicium et justitiam : ut adducat Dominus propter Abraham omnia quæ locutus est ad eum.
     EN: For I know that he will command his children and his household after him to keep the way of the Lord and practice justice and righteousness, so that the Lord may bring about for Abraham everything He has spoken to him.”

18:20  LA: Dixit itaque Dominus : Clamor Sodomorum et Gomorrhæ multiplicatus est, et peccatum eorum aggravatum est nimis.
     EN: The Lord said, “The outcry of Sodom and Gomorrah has grown great, and their sin has become exceedingly grievous.

18:21  LA: Descendam, et videbo utrum clamorem qui venit ad me, opere compleverint ; an non est ita, ut sciam.
     EN: I will go down and see whether their deeds fulfill the outcry that has come to Me, or whether it is not so, that I may know.”

18:22  LA: Converteruntque se inde, et abierunt Sodomam : Abraham vero adhuc stabat coram Domino.
     EN: They turned from there and went to Sodom, but Abraham still stood before the Lord.

18:23  LA: Et appropinquans ait : Numquid perdes justum cum impio ?
     EN: He drew near and said, “Will You destroy the righteous with the wicked?

18:24  LA: si fuerint quinquaginta justi in civitate, peribunt simul ? et non parces loco illi propter quinquaginta justos, si fuerint in eo ?
     EN: If there are fifty righteous people in the city, shall they perish together? Will You not spare that place for the sake of the fifty righteous people, if they are in it?

18:25  LA: Absit a te ut rem hanc facias, et occidas justum cum impio, fiatque justus sicut impius, non est hoc tuum : qui judicas omnem terram, nequaquam facies judicium hoc.
     EN: Far be it from You to do this thing, to kill the righteous with the wicked so that the righteous fare like the wicked. This is not Your way. You who judge the whole earth will surely not render such a judgment.”

18:26  LA: Dixitque Dominus ad eum : Si invenero Sodomis quinquaginta justos in medio civitatis, dimittam omni loco propter eos.
     EN: The Lord said to him, “If I find fifty righteous people in the midst of Sodom, I will spare the whole place for their sake.”

18:27  LA: Respondensque Abraham, ait : Quia semel cœpi, loquar ad Dominum meum, cum sim pulvis et cinis.
     EN: Abraham answered, “Since I have begun, I will speak to my Lord, though I am dust and ashes.

18:28  LA: Quid si minus quinquaginta justis quinque fuerint ? delebis, propter quadraginta quinque, universam urbem ? Et ait : Non delebo, si invenero ibi quadraginta quinque.
     EN: What if there are five fewer than fifty righteous people? Will You destroy the whole city because there are forty-five?” He said, “I will not destroy it if I find forty-five there.”

18:29  LA: Rursumque locutus est ad eum : Sin autem quadraginta ibi inventi fuerint, quid facies ? Ait : Non percutiam propter quadraginta.
     EN: He spoke to Him again: “But if forty are found there, what will You do?” He said, “I will not strike it for the sake of the forty.”

18:30  LA: Ne quæso, inquit, indigneris, Domine, si loquar : quid si ibi inventi fuerint triginta ? Respondit : Non faciam, si invenero ibi triginta.
     EN: “Please do not be angry, Lord,” he said, “if I speak. What if thirty are found there?” He answered, “I will not do it if I find thirty there.”

18:31  LA: Quia semel, ait, cœpi loquar ad Dominum meum : quid si ibi inventi fuerint viginti ? Ait : Non interficiam propter viginti.
     EN: “Since I have begun,” he said, “I will speak to my Lord. What if twenty are found there?” He said, “I will not kill them for the sake of the twenty.”

18:32  LA: Obsecro, inquit, ne irascaris, Domine, si loquar adhuc semel : quid si inventi fuerint ibi decem ? Et dixit : Non delebo propter decem.
     EN: “I beg You,” he said, “do not be angry, Lord, if I speak once more. What if ten are found there?” He said, “I will not destroy it for the sake of the ten.”

18:33  LA: Abiitque Dominus, postquam cessavit loqui ad Abraham : et ille reversus est in locum suum.
     EN: The Lord departed after He finished speaking to Abraham, and Abraham returned to his place.

GENESIS 19

19:1  LA: Veneruntque duo angeli Sodomam vespere, et sedente Lot in foribus civitatis. Qui cum vidisset eos, surrexit, et ivit obviam eis : adoravitque pronus in terram,
     EN: The two angels came to Sodom in the evening, while Lot sat at the city gates. Seeing them, he rose, went to meet them, and bowed facedown to the ground.

19:2  LA: et dixit : Obsecro, domini, declinate in domum pueri vestri, et manete ibi : lavate pedes vestros, et mane proficiscemini in viam vestram. Qui dixerunt : Minime, sed in platea manebimus.
     EN: He said, “Please, my lords, turn aside to your servant's house and stay there. Wash your feet, and in the morning you may go on your way.” They said, “No; we will stay in the square.”

19:3  LA: Compulit illos oppido ut diverterent ad eum : ingressisque domum illius fecit convivium, et coxit azyma, et comederunt.
     EN: He pressed them earnestly to turn aside to him. When they entered his house, he made them a feast and baked unleavened bread, and they ate.

19:4  LA: Prius autem quam irent cubitum, viri civitatis vallaverunt domum a puero usque ad senem, omnis populus simul.
     EN: Before they went to bed, the city's men surrounded the house, from boys to old men, all the people together.

19:5  LA: Vocaveruntque Lot, et dixerunt ei : Ubi sunt viri qui introierunt ad te nocte ? educ illos huc, ut cognoscamus eos.
     EN: They called Lot and said to him, “Where are the men who came to you tonight? Bring them out here, so that we may know them.”

19:6  LA: Egressus ad eos Lot, post tergum occludens ostium, ait :
     EN: Lot went out to them, closing the door behind him, and said,

19:7  LA: Nolite, quæso, fratres mei, nolite malum hoc facere.
     EN: “Please, my brothers, do not do this evil.

19:8  LA: Habeo duas filias, quæ necdum cognoverunt virum : educam eas ad vos, et abutimini eis sicut vobis placuerit, dummodo viris istis nihil mali faciatis, quia ingressi sunt sub umbra culminis mei.
     EN: I have two daughters who have not yet known a man. I will bring them out to you, and you may use them as you please, provided you do no evil to these men, because they have come under the shelter of my roof.”

19:9  LA: At illi dixerunt : Recede illuc. Et rursus : Ingressus es, inquiunt, ut advena ; numquid ut judices ? te ergo ipsum magis quam hos affligemus. Vimque faciebant Lot vehementissime : jamque prope erat ut effringerent fores.
     EN: They said, “Stand back!” Then they said, “You came as a stranger; did you come to judge? We will treat you worse than them.” They pressed violently against Lot and were nearly breaking down the door.

19:10  LA: Et ecce miserunt manum viri, et introduxerunt ad se Lot, clauseruntque ostium :
     EN: Then the men stretched out their hands, pulled Lot in to them, and shut the door.

19:11  LA: et eos, qui foris erant, percusserunt cæcitate a minimo usque ad maximum, ita ut ostium invenire non possent.
     EN: They struck those outside with blindness, from the least to the greatest, so that they could not find the door.

19:12  LA: Dixerunt autem ad Lot : Habes hic quempiam tuorum ? generum, aut filios, aut filias, omnes, qui tui sunt, educ de urbe hac :
     EN: They said to Lot, “Have you anyone belonging to you here—a son-in-law, sons, or daughters? Bring everyone who belongs to you out of this city.

19:13  LA: delebimus enim locum istum, eo quod increverit clamor eorum coram Domino, qui misit nos ut perdamus illos.
     EN: For we will destroy this place, because the outcry against them has grown great before the Lord, who sent us to destroy them.”

19:14  LA: Egressus itaque Lot, locutus est ad generos suos qui accepturi erant filias ejus, et dixit : Surgite, egredimini de loco isto : quia delebit Dominus civitatem hanc. Et visus est eis quasi ludens loqui.
     EN: Lot went out and spoke to his sons-in-law, who were to marry his daughters, saying, “Rise and leave this place, for the Lord will destroy this city.” But they thought he spoke in jest.

19:15  LA: Cumque esset mane, cogebant eum angeli, dicentes : Surge, tolle uxorem tuam, et duas filias quas habes : ne et tu pariter pereas in scelere civitatis.
     EN: When morning came, the angels urged him, saying, “Rise, take your wife and the two daughters you have, lest you too perish in the city's wickedness.”

19:16  LA: Dissimulante illo, apprehenderunt manum ejus, et manum uxoris, ac duarum filiarum ejus, eo quod parceret Dominus illi.
     EN: As he delayed, they took his hand, his wife's hand, and the hands of his two daughters, because the Lord spared him.

19:17  LA: Eduxeruntque eum, et posuerunt extra civitatem : ibique locuti sunt ad eum, dicentes : Salva animam tuam : noli respicere post tergum, nec stes in omni circa regione : sed in monte salvum te fac, ne et tu simul pereas.
     EN: They brought him out and set him outside the city, and there they spoke to him, saying, “Save your life. Do not look behind you or stop anywhere in the surrounding region. Escape to the mountain, lest you too perish.”

19:18  LA: Dixitque Lot ad eos : Quæso, domine mi,
     EN: Lot said to them, “Please, my lord,

19:19  LA: quia invenit servus tuus gratiam coram te, et magnificasti misericordiam tuam quam fecisti mecum, ut salvares animam meam, nec possum in monte salvari, ne forte apprehendat me malum, et moriar :
     EN: since your servant has found favor before you and you have shown me great mercy by saving my life, I cannot escape to the mountain, lest disaster overtake me and I die.

19:20  LA: est civitas hæc juxta, ad quam possum fugere, parva, et salvabor in ea : numquid non modica est, et vivet anima mea ?
     EN: There is this nearby city to which I can flee, a small one, and I shall be safe there. Is it not little? And my life will be preserved.”

19:21  LA: Dixitque ad eum : Ecce etiam in hoc suscepi preces tuas, ut non subvertam urbem pro qua locutus es.
     EN: He said to him, “See, in this too I have accepted your prayers, and will not overthrow the city of which you have spoken.

19:22  LA: Festina, et salvare ibi : quia non potero facere quidquam donec ingrediaris illuc. Idcirco vocatum est nomen urbis illius Segor.
     EN: Hurry and escape there, for I can do nothing until you enter it.” Therefore that city was named Zoar.

19:23  LA: Sol egressus est super terram, et Lot ingressus est Segor.
     EN: The sun had risen over the earth when Lot entered Zoar.

19:24  LA: Igitur Dominus pluit super Sodomam et Gomorrham sulphur et ignem a Domino de cælo :
     EN: Then the Lord rained sulfur and fire upon Sodom and Gomorrah, from the Lord out of heaven.

19:25  LA: et subvertit civitates has, et omnem circa regionem, universos habitatores urbium, et cuncta terræ virentia.
     EN: He overthrew these cities, the whole surrounding region, all the cities' inhabitants, and everything growing upon the ground.

19:26  LA: Respiciensque uxor ejus post se, versa est in statuam salis.
     EN: His wife looked behind her and was turned into a pillar of salt.

19:27  LA: Abraham autem consurgens mane, ubi steterat prius cum Domino,
     EN: Abraham rose early and came to the place where he had previously stood with the Lord.

19:28  LA: intuitus est Sodomam et Gomorrham, et universam terram regionis illius : viditque ascendentem favillam de terra quasi fornacis fumum.
     EN: He looked toward Sodom and Gomorrah and the whole land of that region, and saw ashes rising from the earth like smoke from a furnace.

19:29  LA: Cum enim subverteret Deus civitates regionis illius, recordatus Abrahæ, liberavit Lot de subversione urbium in quibus habitaverat.
     EN: For when God overthrew the cities of that region, He remembered Abraham and delivered Lot from the overthrow of the cities in which he had lived.

19:30  LA: Ascenditque Lot de Segor, et mansit in monte, duæ quoque filiæ ejus cum eo (timuerat enim manere in Segor) et mansit in spelunca ipse, et duæ filiæ ejus cum eo.
     EN: Lot went up from Zoar and stayed in the mountain, with his two daughters, for he had been afraid to remain in Zoar. He lived in a cave, he and his two daughters with him.

19:31  LA: Dixitque major ad minorem : Pater noster senex est, et nullus virorum remansit in terra, qui possit ingredi ad nos juxta morem universæ terræ.
     EN: The elder said to the younger, “Our father is old, and no man remains in the land who can come in to us in the manner of all the earth.

19:32  LA: Veni, inebriemus eum vino, dormiamusque cum eo, ut servare possimus ex patre nostro semen.
     EN: Come, let us make him drunk with wine and sleep with him, so that we may preserve offspring from our father.”

19:33  LA: Dederunt itaque patri suo bibere vinum nocte illa. Et ingressa est major, dormivitque cum patre ; at ille non sensit, nec quando accubuit filia, nec quando surrexit.
     EN: That night they gave their father wine to drink. The elder went in and slept with her father; he did not perceive when his daughter lay down or when she rose.

19:34  LA: Altera quoque die dixit major ad minorem : Ecce dormivi heri cum patre meo, demus ei bibere vinum etiam hac nocte, et dormies cum eo, ut salvemus semen de patre nostro.
     EN: The next day the elder said to the younger, “See, yesterday I slept with my father. Let us give him wine to drink again tonight, and you shall sleep with him, so that we may preserve offspring from our father.”

19:35  LA: Dederunt etiam et illa nocte patri suo bibere vinum, ingressaque minor filia, dormivit cum eo : et ne tunc quidem sensit quando concubuerit, vel quando illa surrexerit.
     EN: That night also they gave their father wine to drink, and the younger daughter went in and slept with him. Again he did not perceive when she lay down or when she rose.

19:36  LA: Conceperunt ergo duæ filiæ Lot de patre suo.
     EN: Thus Lot's two daughters conceived by their father.

19:37  LA: Peperitque major filium, et vocavit nomen ejus Moab : ipse est pater Moabitarum usque in præsentem diem.
     EN: The elder bore a son and named him Moab; he is the father of the Moabites to the present day.

19:38  LA: Minor quoque peperit filium, et vocavit nomen ejus Ammon, id est, Filius populi mei : ipse est pater Ammonitarum usque hodie.
     EN: The younger also bore a son and named him Ammon, that is, Son of My People; he is the father of the Ammonites to this day.

GENESIS 20

20:1  LA: Profectus inde Abraham in terram australem, habitavit inter Cades et Sur : et peregrinatus est in Geraris.
     EN: Abraham journeyed from there to the southern land and lived between Kadesh and Shur, staying as a stranger in Gerar.

20:2  LA: Dixitque de Sara uxore suo : Soror mea est. Misit ergo Abimelech rex Geraræ, et tulit eam.
     EN: He said of his wife Sarah, “She is my sister.” So Abimelech king of Gerar sent and took her.

20:3  LA: Venit autem Deus ad Abimelech per somnium nocte, et ait illi : En morieris propter mulierem quam tulisti : habet enim virum.
     EN: But God came to Abimelech in a dream at night and said to him, “See, you shall die because of the woman you have taken, for she has a husband.”

20:4  LA: Abimelech vero non tetigerat eam, et ait : Domine, num gentem ignorantem et justam interficies ?
     EN: Abimelech had not touched her, and said, “Lord, will You kill an unknowing and righteous nation?

20:5  LA: nonne ipse dixit mihi : Soror mea est : et ipsa ait : Frater meus est ? In simplicitate cordis mei, et munditia manuum mearum feci hoc.
     EN: Did he not tell me, ‘She is my sister,’ and did she herself not say, ‘He is my brother’? In innocence of heart and cleanness of hands I did this.”

20:6  LA: Dixitque ad eum Deus : Et ego scio quod simplici corde feceris : et ideo custodivi te ne peccares in me, et non dimisi ut tangeres eam.
     EN: God said to him, “I too know that you acted with an innocent heart. Therefore I kept you from sinning against Me and did not allow you to touch her.

20:7  LA: Nunc ergo redde viro suo uxorem, quia propheta est : et orabit pro te, et vives : si autem nolueris reddere, scito quod morte morieris tu, et omnia quæ tua sunt.
     EN: Now return the man's wife, for he is a prophet. He will pray for you, and you shall live. But if you refuse to return her, know that you and all that belongs to you shall surely die.”

20:8  LA: Statimque de nocte consurgens Abimelech, vocavit omnes servos suos : et locutus est universa verba hæc in auribus eorum, timueruntque omnes viri valde.
     EN: Abimelech immediately rose in the night, called all his servants, and spoke all these words in their hearing. All the men were greatly afraid.

20:9  LA: Vocavit autem Abimelech etiam Abraham, et dixit ei : Quid fecisti nobis ? quid peccavimus in te, quia induxisti super me et super regnum meum peccatum grande ? quæ non debuisti facere, fecisti nobis.
     EN: Abimelech also called Abraham and said to him, “What have you done to us? How have we sinned against you, that you have brought a great sin upon me and my kingdom? You have done to us things you ought not to have done.”

20:10  LA: Rursumque expostulans, ait : Quid vidisti, ut hoc faceres ?
     EN: Again he demanded, “What did you see that led you to do this?”

20:11  LA: Respondit Abraham : Cogitavi mecum, dicens : Forsitan non est timor Dei in loco isto : et interficient me propter uxorem meam :
     EN: Abraham answered, “I thought to myself, ‘Perhaps there is no fear of God in this place, and they will kill me because of my wife.’

20:12  LA: alias autem et vere soror mea est, filia patris mei, et non filia matris meæ, et duxi eam in uxorem.
     EN: Besides, she truly is my sister, the daughter of my father but not the daughter of my mother, and I took her as my wife.

20:13  LA: Postquam autem eduxit me Deus de domo patris mei, dixi ad eam : Hanc misericordiam facies mecum : in omni loco, ad quem ingrediemur, dices quod frater tuus sim.
     EN: When God led me out of my father's house, I said to her, ‘Show me this kindness: in every place we enter, say that I am your brother.’”

20:14  LA: Tulit igitur Abimelech oves et boves, et servos et ancillas, et dedit Abraham : reddiditque illi Saram uxorem suam,
     EN: Abimelech took sheep, oxen, male servants, and maidservants and gave them to Abraham, and returned his wife Sarah to him.

20:15  LA: et ait : Terra coram vobis est, ubicumque tibi placuerit habita.
     EN: He said, “The land lies before you; live wherever you please.”

20:16  LA: Saræ autem dixit : Ecce mille argenteos dedi fratri tuo, hoc erit tibi in velamen oculorum ad omnes qui tecum sunt, et quocumque perrexeris : mementoque te deprehensam.
     EN: To Sarah he said, “See, I have given your brother a thousand silver pieces. This shall be a covering for your eyes before all who are with you and wherever you go; and remember that you have been found out.”

20:17  LA: Orante autem Abraham, sanavit Deus Abimelech et uxorem, ancillasque ejus, et pepererunt :
     EN: Abraham prayed, and God healed Abimelech, his wife, and his maidservants, and they bore children.

20:18  LA: concluserat enim Dominus omnem vulvam domus Abimelech propter Saram uxorem Abrahæ.
     EN: For the Lord had closed every womb in Abimelech's household because of Sarah, Abraham's wife.

GENESIS 21

21:1  LA: Visitavit autem Dominus Saram, sicut promiserat : et implevit quæ locutus est.
     EN: The Lord visited Sarah as He had promised, and fulfilled what He had spoken.

21:2  LA: Concepitque et peperit filium in senectute sua, tempore quo prædixerat ei Deus.
     EN: She conceived and bore a son in her old age, at the time God had foretold to her.

21:3  LA: Vocavitque Abraham nomen filii sui, quem genuit ei Sara, Isaac :
     EN: Abraham named his son, whom Sarah bore him, Isaac,

21:4  LA: et circumcidit eum octavo die, sicut præceperat ei Deus,
     EN: and circumcised him on the eighth day, as God had commanded him,

21:5  LA: cum centum esset annorum : hac quippe ætate patris, natus est Isaac.
     EN: when he was one hundred years old; for Isaac was born when his father was this age.

21:6  LA: Dixitque Sara : Risum fecit mihi Deus : quicumque audierit, corridebit mihi.
     EN: Sarah said, “God has made laughter for me. Whoever hears will laugh with me.”

21:7  LA: Rursumque ait : Quis auditurus crederet Abraham quod Sara lactaret filium, quem peperit ei jam seni ?
     EN: Again she said, “Who would have believed Abraham, on hearing it, that Sarah would nurse a son whom she bore him in his old age?”

21:8  LA: Crevit igitur puer, et ablactatus est : fecitque Abraham grande convivium in die ablactationis ejus.
     EN: The child grew and was weaned, and Abraham made a great feast on the day of his weaning.

21:9  LA: Cumque vidisset Sara filium Agar Ægyptiæ ludentem cum Isaac filio suo, dixit ad Abraham :
     EN: Sarah saw the son of Hagar the Egyptian playing with her son Isaac, and said to Abraham,

21:10  LA: Ejice ancillam hanc, et filium ejus : non enim erit hæres filius ancillæ cum filio meo Isaac.
     EN: “Cast out this maidservant and her son, for the maidservant's son shall not be heir with my son Isaac.”

21:11  LA: Dure accepit hoc Abraham pro filio suo.
     EN: Abraham took this hard because of his son.

21:12  LA: Cui dixit Deus : Non tibi videatur asperum super puero, et super ancilla tua : omnia quæ dixerit tibi Sara, audi vocem ejus : quia in Isaac vocabitur tibi semen.
     EN: God said to him, “Do not let this seem harsh to you concerning the boy and your maidservant. In everything Sarah tells you, listen to her voice, for through Isaac your offspring shall be named.

21:13  LA: Sed et filium ancillæ faciam in gentem magnam, quia semen tuum est.
     EN: But I will also make the maidservant's son a great nation, because he is your offspring.”

21:14  LA: Surrexit itaque Abraham mane, et tollens panem et utrem aquæ, imposuit scapulæ ejus, tradiditque puerum, et dimisit eam. Quæ cum abiisset, errabat in solitudine Bersabee.
     EN: Abraham rose early, took bread and a skin of water, placed them upon her shoulder, entrusted the boy to her, and sent her away. She departed and wandered in the wilderness of Beersheba.

21:15  LA: Cumque consumpta esset aqua in utre, abjecit puerum subter unam arborum, quæ ibi erant.
     EN: When the water in the skin was exhausted, she cast the boy down beneath one of the trees there.

21:16  LA: Et abiit, seditque e regione procul quantum potest arcus jacere : dixit enim : Non videbo morientem puerum : et sedens contra, levavit vocem suam et flevit.
     EN: She went and sat opposite him at a distance, about a bowshot away, for she said, “I will not watch the boy die.” Sitting opposite him, she raised her voice and wept.

21:17  LA: Exaudivit autem Deus vocem pueri : vocavitque angelus Dei Agar de cælo, dicens : Quid agis Agar ? noli timere : exaudivit enim Deus vocem pueri de loco in quo est.
     EN: God heard the boy's voice. The angel of God called to Hagar from heaven, saying, “What are you doing, Hagar? Do not fear, for God has heard the boy's voice from the place where he is.

21:18  LA: Surge, tolle puerum, et tene manum illius : quia in gentem magnam faciam eum.
     EN: Rise, lift the boy, and hold his hand, for I will make him a great nation.”

21:19  LA: Aperuitque oculos ejus Deus : quæ videns puteum aquæ, abiit, et implevit utrem, deditque puero bibere.
     EN: God opened her eyes, and she saw a well of water. She went, filled the skin, and gave the boy a drink.

21:20  LA: Et fuit cum eo : qui crevit, et moratus est in solitudine, factusque est juvenis sagittarius.
     EN: God was with him. He grew, lived in the wilderness, and became a young archer.

21:21  LA: Habitavitque in deserto Pharan, et accepit illi mater sua uxorem de terra Ægypti.
     EN: He lived in the desert of Paran, and his mother took a wife for him from the land of Egypt.

21:22  LA: Eodem tempore dixit Abimelech, et Phicol princeps exercitus ejus, ad Abraham : Deus tecum est in universis quæ agis.
     EN: At that time Abimelech and Phicol, commander of his army, said to Abraham, “God is with you in everything you do.

21:23  LA: Jura ergo per Deum, ne noceas mihi, et posteris meis, stirpique meæ : sed juxta misericordiam, quam feci tibi, facies mihi, et terræ in qua versatus es advena.
     EN: Therefore swear by God that you will not harm me, my posterity, or my offspring, but will show me and the land in which you have lived as a stranger the same kindness I have shown you.”

21:24  LA: Dixitque Abraham : Ego jurabo.
     EN: Abraham said, “I will swear.”

21:25  LA: Et increpavit Abimelech propter puteum aquæ quem vi abstulerunt servi ejus.
     EN: He reproved Abimelech concerning a well of water that his servants had seized by force.

21:26  LA: Responditque Abimelech : Nescivi quis fecerit hanc rem : sed et tu non indicasti mihi, et ego non audivi præter hodie.
     EN: Abimelech answered, “I did not know who had done this. You did not tell me, and I have not heard of it until today.”

21:27  LA: Tulit itaque Abraham oves et boves, et dedit Abimelech : percusseruntque ambo fœdus.
     EN: Abraham took sheep and oxen and gave them to Abimelech, and the two made a covenant.

21:28  LA: Et statuit Abraham septem agnas gregis seorsum.
     EN: Abraham set seven ewe lambs from the flock apart.

21:29  LA: Cui dixit Abimelech : Quid sibi volunt septem agnæ istæ, quas stare fecisti seorsum ?
     EN: Abimelech said to him, “What do these seven ewe lambs that you have set apart mean?”

21:30  LA: At ille : Septem, inquit, agnas accipies de manu mea : ut sint mihi in testimonium, quoniam ego fodi puteum istum.
     EN: He said, “You shall accept seven ewe lambs from my hand as testimony for me that I dug this well.”

21:31  LA: Idcirco vocatus est locus ille Bersabee : quia ibi uterque juravit.
     EN: Therefore that place was called Beersheba, because both swore there.

21:32  LA: Et inierunt fœdus pro puteo juramenti.
     EN: They made a covenant concerning the Well of the Oath.

21:33  LA: Surrexit autem Abimelech, et Phicol princeps exercitus ejus, reversique sunt in terram Palæstinorum. Abraham vero plantavit nemus in Bersabee, et invocavit ibi nomen Domini Dei æterni.
     EN: Abimelech and Phicol, commander of his army, rose and returned to the land of the Philistines. Abraham planted a grove in Beersheba and called there upon the name of the Lord, the everlasting God.

21:34  LA: Et fuit colonus terræ Palæstinorum diebus multis.
     EN: He lived as a settler in the land of the Philistines for many days.

GENESIS 22

22:1  LA: Quæ postquam gesta sunt, tentavit Deus Abraham, et dixit ad eum : Abraham, Abraham. At ille respondit : Adsum.
     EN: After these things, God tested Abraham and said to him, “Abraham, Abraham.” He answered, “Here I am.”

22:2  LA: Ait illi : Tolle filium tuum unigenitum, quem diligis, Isaac, et vade in terram visionis, atque ibi offeres eum in holocaustum super unum montium quem monstravero tibi.
     EN: He said to him, “Take your only-begotten son, whom you love, Isaac, and go to the land of vision. There you shall offer him as a whole burnt offering upon one of the mountains I will show you.”

22:3  LA: Igitur Abraham de nocte consurgens, stravit asinum suum, ducens secum duos juvenes, et Isaac filium suum : cumque concidisset ligna in holocaustum, abiit ad locum quem præceperat ei Deus.
     EN: Abraham rose in the night, saddled his donkey, and took with him two young servants and his son Isaac. He split wood for the whole burnt offering and set out for the place God had commanded him.

22:4  LA: Die autem tertio, elevatis oculis, vidit locum procul :
     EN: On the third day he raised his eyes and saw the place in the distance.

22:5  LA: dixitque ad pueros suos : Expectate hic cum asino : ego et puer illuc usque properantes, postquam adoraverimus, revertemur ad vos.
     EN: He said to his servants, “Wait here with the donkey. The boy and I will go over there, and after we have worshiped, we will return to you.”

22:6  LA: Tulit quoque ligna holocausti, et imposuit super Isaac filium suum : ipse vero portabat in manibus ignem et gladium. Cumque duo pergerent simul,
     EN: He took the wood for the whole burnt offering and laid it upon his son Isaac, while he himself carried the fire and the sword in his hands. As the two walked together,

22:7  LA: dixit Isaac patri suo : Pater mi. At ille respondit : Quid vis, fili ? Ecce, inquit, ignis et ligna : ubi est victima holocausti ?
     EN: Isaac said to his father, “My father.” He answered, “What is it, son?” “Here are the fire and the wood,” he said, “but where is the victim for the whole burnt offering?”

22:8  LA: Dixit autem Abraham : Deus providebit sibi victimam holocausti, fili mi. Pergebant ergo pariter.
     EN: Abraham said, “God will provide for Himself the victim for the whole burnt offering, my son.” So they went on together.

22:9  LA: Et venerunt ad locum quem ostenderat ei Deus, in quo ædificavit altare, et desuper ligna composuit ; cumque alligasset Isaac filium suum, posuit eum in altare super struem lignorum.
     EN: They came to the place God had shown him. There he built an altar and arranged the wood upon it. He bound his son Isaac and placed him upon the altar on the pile of wood.

22:10  LA: Extenditque manum, et arripuit gladium, ut immolaret filium suum.
     EN: He stretched out his hand and grasped the sword to sacrifice his son.

22:11  LA: Et ecce angelus Domini de cælo clamavit, dicens : Abraham, Abraham. Qui respondit : Adsum.
     EN: Then the angel of the Lord called from heaven, saying, “Abraham, Abraham.” He answered, “Here I am.”

22:12  LA: Dixitque ei : Non extendas manum tuam super puerum, neque facias illi quidquam : nunc cognovi quod times Deum, et non pepercisti unigenito filio tuo propter me.
     EN: He said to him, “Do not stretch out your hand against the boy or do anything to him. Now I know that you fear God, and have not spared your only-begotten son for My sake.”

22:13  LA: Levavit Abraham oculos suos, viditque post tergum arietem inter vepres hærentem cornibus, quem assumens obtulit holocaustum pro filio.
     EN: Abraham raised his eyes and saw behind him a ram caught by its horns among the thorns. He took it and offered it as a whole burnt offering in place of his son.

22:14  LA: Appellavitque nomen loci illius, Dominus videt. Unde usque hodie dicitur : In monte Dominus videbit.
     EN: He named that place The Lord Sees. Hence the saying to this day, “On the mountain the Lord will see.”

22:15  LA: Vocavit autem angelus Domini Abraham secundo de cælo, dicens :
     EN: The angel of the Lord called Abraham a second time from heaven, saying,

22:16  LA: Per memetipsum juravi, dicit Dominus : quia fecisti hanc rem, et non pepercisti filio tuo unigenito propter me :
     EN: “By Myself I have sworn, says the Lord: because you have done this and have not spared your only-begotten son for My sake,

22:17  LA: benedicam tibi, et multiplicabo semen tuum sicut stellas cæli, et velut arenam quæ est in littore maris : possidebit semen tuum portas inimicorum suorum,
     EN: I will bless you and multiply your offspring like the stars of heaven and like the sand upon the seashore. Your offspring shall possess the gates of their enemies,

22:18  LA: et benedicentur in semine tuo omnes gentes terræ, quia obedisti voci meæ.
     EN: and in your offspring all the nations of the earth shall be blessed, because you obeyed My voice.”

22:19  LA: Reversus est Abraham ad pueros suos, abieruntque Bersabee simul, et habitavit ibi.
     EN: Abraham returned to his servants, and they went together to Beersheba, where he lived.

22:20  LA: His ita gestis, nuntiatum est Abrahæ quod Melcha quoque genuisset filios Nachor fratri suo :
     EN: After these things, Abraham was told that Milcah also had borne children to his brother Nahor:

22:21  LA: Hus primogenitum, et Buz fratrem ejus, et Camuel patrem Syrorum,
     EN: Uz the firstborn, his brother Buz, Kemuel the father of the Syrians,

22:22  LA: et Cased, et Azau, Pheldas quoque et Jedlaph,
     EN: Chesed, Hazo, Pildash, Jidlaph,

22:23  LA: ac Bathuel, de quo nata est Rebecca : octo istos genuit Melcha, Nachor fratri Abrahæ.
     EN: and Bethuel, of whom Rebekah was born. Milcah bore these eight to Nahor, Abraham's brother.

22:24  LA: Concubina vero illius, nomine Roma, peperit Tabee, et Gaham, et Thahas, et Maacha.
     EN: His concubine, named Reumah, bore Tebah, Gaham, Tahash, and Maacah.

GENESIS 23

23:1  LA: Vixit autem Sara centum viginti septem annis.
     EN: Sarah lived one hundred twenty-seven years.

23:2  LA: Et mortua est in civitate Arbee, quæ est Hebron, in terra Chanaan : venitque Abraham ut plangeret et fleret eam.
     EN: She died in the city of Arba, which is Hebron, in the land of Canaan. Abraham came to mourn and weep for her.

23:3  LA: Cumque surrexisset ab officio funeris, locutus est ad filios Heth, dicens :
     EN: When he rose from his mourning duties, he spoke to the sons of Heth, saying,

23:4  LA: Advena sum et peregrinus apud vos : date mihi jus sepulchri vobiscum, ut sepeliam mortuum meum.
     EN: “I am a stranger and sojourner among you. Grant me the right to a burial place among you, so that I may bury my dead.”

23:5  LA: Responderunt filii Heth, dicentes :
     EN: The sons of Heth answered,

23:6  LA: Audi nos, domine : princeps Dei es apud nos : in electis sepulchris nostris sepeli mortuum tuum, nullusque te prohibere poterit quin in monumento ejus sepelias mortuum tuum.
     EN: “Hear us, my lord. You are a prince of God among us. Bury your dead in the choicest of our tombs; none of us can prevent you from burying your dead in his tomb.”

23:7  LA: Surrexit Abraham, et adoravit populum terræ, filios videlicet Heth :
     EN: Abraham rose and bowed to the people of the land, the sons of Heth.

23:8  LA: dixitque ad eos : Si placet animæ vestræ ut sepeliam mortuum meum, audite me, et intercedite pro me apud Ephron filium Seor :
     EN: He said to them, “If you are willing that I should bury my dead, hear me and intercede for me with Ephron son of Zohar,

23:9  LA: ut det mihi speluncam duplicem, quam habet in extrema parte agri sui : pecunia digna tradat eam mihi coram vobis in possessionem sepulchri.
     EN: that he may give me the double cave he owns at the edge of his field. Let him transfer it to me before you for a fair price, as a possession for burial.”

23:10  LA: Habitabat autem Ephron in medio filiorum Heth. Responditque Ephron ad Abraham, cunctis audientibus qui ingrediebantur portam civitatis illius, dicens :
     EN: Ephron was dwelling among the sons of Heth. Ephron answered Abraham in the hearing of everyone entering his city's gate, saying,

23:11  LA: Nequaquam ita fiat, domine mi, sed tu magis ausculta quod loquor. Agrum trado tibi, et speluncam quæ in eo est, præsentibus filiis populi mei ; sepeli mortuum tuum.
     EN: “Not so, my lord; instead hear what I say. I give you the field and the cave in it, in the presence of the sons of my people. Bury your dead.”

23:12  LA: Adoravit Abraham coram populo terræ.
     EN: Abraham bowed before the people of the land,

23:13  LA: Et locutus est ad Ephron circumstante plebe : Quæso ut audias me : dabo pecuniam pro agro : suscipe eam, et sic sepeliam mortuum meum in eo.
     EN: and spoke to Ephron with the people standing around: “Please hear me. I will give the price of the field; accept it, and then I shall bury my dead in it.”

23:14  LA: Responditque Ephron :
     EN: Ephron answered,

23:15  LA: Domine mi, audi me : terra, quam postulas, quadringentis siclis argenti valet : istud est pretium inter me et te : sed quantum est hoc ? sepeli mortuum tuum.
     EN: “My lord, hear me. The land you ask for is worth four hundred shekels of silver. This is the price between you and me, but what is that? Bury your dead.”

23:16  LA: Quod cum audisset Abraham, appendit pecuniam, quam Ephron postulaverat, audientibus filiis Heth, quadringentos siclos argenti probatæ monetæ publicæ.
     EN: When Abraham heard this, he weighed out the money Ephron had asked for in the hearing of the sons of Heth: four hundred shekels of silver of the accepted public standard.

23:17  LA: Confirmatusque est ager quondam Ephronis, in quo erat spelunca duplex, respiciens Mambre, tam ipse, quam spelunca, et omnes arbores ejus in cunctis terminis ejus per circuitum,
     EN: Thus the field formerly belonging to Ephron, in which was the double cave facing Mamre—the field itself, the cave, and all its trees within all its surrounding boundaries—

23:18  LA: Abrahæ in possessionem, videntibus filiis Heth, et cunctis qui intrabant portam civitatis illius.
     EN: was confirmed as Abraham's possession in the sight of the sons of Heth and everyone entering that city's gate.

23:19  LA: Atque ita sepelivit Abraham Saram uxorem suam in spelunca agri duplici, quæ respiciebat Mambre. Hæc est Hebron in terra Chanaan.
     EN: So Abraham buried his wife Sarah in the double cave of the field facing Mamre, which is Hebron in the land of Canaan.

23:20  LA: Et confirmatus est ager, et antrum quod erat in eo, Abrahæ in possessionem monumenti a filiis Heth.
     EN: The sons of Heth confirmed the field and the cave in it as Abraham's possession for a burial place.

GENESIS 24

24:1  LA: Erat autem Abraham senex, dierumque multorum : et Dominus in cunctis benedixerat ei.
     EN: Abraham was old and advanced in days, and the Lord had blessed him in everything.

24:2  LA: Dixitque ad servum seniorem domus suæ, qui præerat omnibus quæ habebat : Pone manum tuam subter femur meum,
     EN: He said to the senior servant of his household, who was in charge of all he had, “Place your hand beneath my thigh,

24:3  LA: ut adjurem te per Dominum Deum cæli et terræ, ut non accipias uxorem filio meo de filiabus Chananæorum, inter quos habito :
     EN: so that I may make you swear by the Lord, the God of heaven and earth, that you will not take a wife for my son from the daughters of the Canaanites among whom I live,

24:4  LA: sed ad terram et cognationem meam proficiscaris et inde accipias uxorem filio meo Isaac.
     EN: but will go to my land and my kindred and take a wife from there for my son Isaac.”

24:5  LA: Respondit servus : Si noluerit mulier venire mecum in terram hanc, numquid reducere debeo filium tuum ad locum, de quo egressus es ?
     EN: The servant answered, “If the woman is unwilling to come with me to this land, should I take your son back to the place from which you came?”

24:6  LA: Dixitque Abraham : Cave nequando reducas filium meum illuc.
     EN: Abraham said, “Take care never to bring my son back there.

24:7  LA: Dominus Deus cæli, qui tulit me de domo patris mei, et de terra nativitatis meæ, qui locutus est mihi, et juravit mihi, dicens : Semini tuo dabo terram hanc : ipse mittet angelum suum coram te, et accipies inde uxorem filio meo :
     EN: The Lord, the God of heaven, who took me from my father's house and the land of my birth, who spoke to me and swore to me, saying, ‘To your offspring I will give this land’—He will send His angel before you, and you shall take a wife for my son from there.

24:8  LA: sin autem mulier noluerit sequi te, non teneberis juramento : filium meum tantum ne reducas illuc.
     EN: But if the woman is unwilling to follow you, you will not be bound by the oath. Only do not bring my son back there.”

24:9  LA: Posuit ergo servus manum sub femore Abraham domini sui, et juravit illi super sermone hoc.
     EN: The servant placed his hand beneath the thigh of Abraham his master and swore to him concerning this matter.

24:10  LA: Tulitque decem camelos de grege domini sui, et abiit, ex omnibus bonis ejus portans secum, profectusque perrexit in Mesopotamiam ad urbem Nachor.
     EN: He took ten camels from his master's herd and departed, carrying with him some of all his master's good things. He journeyed to Mesopotamia, to Nahor's city.

24:11  LA: Cumque camelos fecisset accumbere extra oppidum juxta puteum aquæ vespere, tempore quo solent mulieres egredi ad hauriendam aquam, dixit :
     EN: He made the camels kneel outside the town beside a well of water in the evening, at the time when women usually go out to draw water, and said,

24:12  LA: Domine Deus domini mei Abraham, occurre, obsecro, mihi hodie, et fac misericordiam cum domino meo Abraham.
     EN: “Lord, God of my master Abraham, please come to my aid today and show kindness to my master Abraham.

24:13  LA: Ecce ego sto prope fontem aquæ, et filiæ habitatorum hujus civitatis egredientur ad hauriendam aquam.
     EN: See, I stand beside the spring of water, and the daughters of this city's inhabitants will come out to draw water.

24:14  LA: Igitur puella, cui ego dixero : Inclina hydriam tuam ut bibam : et illa responderit : Bibe, quin et camelis tuis dabo potum : ipsa est quam præparasti servo tuo Isaac : et per hoc intelligam quod feceris misericordiam cum domino meo.
     EN: So the young woman to whom I say, ‘Lower your jar so that I may drink,’ and who answers, ‘Drink, and I will also give your camels water’—she is the one You have prepared for Your servant Isaac. By this I shall know that You have shown kindness to my master.”

24:15  LA: Necdum intra se verba compleverat, et ecce Rebecca egrediebatur, filia Bathuel, filii Melchæ uxoris Nachor fratris Abraham, habens hydriam in scapula sua :
     EN: He had not yet finished these words within himself when Rebekah came out, the daughter of Bethuel, son of Milcah, the wife of Abraham's brother Nahor, carrying a water jar upon her shoulder.

24:16  LA: puella decora nimis, virgoque pulcherrima, et incognita viro : descenderat autem ad fontem, et impleverat hydriam, ac revertebatur.
     EN: The young woman was very graceful, a most beautiful virgin whom no man had known. She had gone down to the spring, filled her jar, and was returning.

24:17  LA: Occurritque ei servus, et ait : Pauxillum aquæ mihi ad bibendum præbe de hydria tua.
     EN: The servant ran to meet her and said, “Please give me a little water to drink from your jar.”

24:18  LA: Quæ respondit : Bibe, domine mi : celeriterque deposuit hydriam super ulnam suam, et dedit ei potum.
     EN: She answered, “Drink, my lord,” and quickly lowered the jar onto her arm and gave him a drink.

24:19  LA: Cumque ille bibisset, adjecit : Quin et camelis tuis hauriam aquam, donec cuncti bibant.
     EN: When he had drunk, she added, “I will also draw water for your camels until they have all drunk.”

24:20  LA: Effundensque hydriam in canalibus, recurrit ad puteum ut hauriret aquam : et haustam omnibus camelis dedit.
     EN: She emptied her jar into the troughs and ran back to the well to draw water, and gave what she drew to all the camels.

24:21  LA: Ipse autem contemplabatur eam tacitus, scire volens utrum prosperum iter suum fecisset Dominus, an non.
     EN: The man watched her silently, wishing to know whether the Lord had made his journey prosper or not.

24:22  LA: Postquam autem biberunt cameli, protulit vir inaures aureas, appendentes siclos duos, et armillas totidem pondo siclorum decem.
     EN: After the camels had drunk, the man brought out gold earrings weighing two shekels and two bracelets weighing ten shekels.

24:23  LA: Dixitque ad eam : Cujus es filia ? indica mihi, est in domo patris tui locus ad manendum ?
     EN: He said to her, “Whose daughter are you? Tell me, is there room in your father's house to stay?”

24:24  LA: Quæ respondit : Filia sum Bathuelis, filii Melchæ, quem peperit ipsi Nachor.
     EN: She answered, “I am the daughter of Bethuel, Milcah's son, whom she bore to Nahor.”

24:25  LA: Et addidit, dicens : Palearum quoque et fœni plurimum est apud nos, et locus spatiosus ad manendum.
     EN: She added, “We have plenty of straw and hay, and ample room to stay.”

24:26  LA: Inclinavit se homo, et adoravit Dominum,
     EN: The man bowed down and worshiped the Lord,

24:27  LA: dicens : Benedictus Dominus Deus domini mei Abraham, qui non abstulit misericordiam et veritatem suam a domino meo, et recto itinere me perduxit in domum fratris domini mei.
     EN: saying, “Blessed be the Lord, the God of my master Abraham, who has not withheld His mercy and faithfulness from my master, and has led me by a straight way to the house of my master's brother.”

24:28  LA: Cucurrit itaque puella, et nuntiavit in domum matris suæ omnia quæ audierat.
     EN: The young woman ran and told her mother's household everything she had heard.

24:29  LA: Habebat autem Rebecca fratrem nomine Laban, qui festinus egressus est ad hominem, ubi erat fons.
     EN: Rebekah had a brother named Laban, who hurried out to the man at the spring.

24:30  LA: Cumque vidisset inaures et armillas in manibus sororis suæ, et audisset cuncta verba referentis : Hæc locutus est mihi homo : venit ad virum qui stabat juxta camelos, et prope fontem aquæ :
     EN: When he saw the earrings and the bracelets on his sister's hands, and heard her recount all the words, saying, “This is what the man said to me,” he came to the man, who stood beside the camels near the spring of water.

24:31  LA: dixitque ad eum : Ingredere, benedicte Domini : cur foris stas ? præparavi domum, et locum camelis.
     EN: He said to him, “Come in, blessed of the Lord. Why do you stand outside? I have prepared the house and a place for the camels.”

24:32  LA: Et introduxit eum in hospitium : ac destravit camelos, deditque paleas et fœnum, et aquam ad lavandos pedes ejus, et virorum qui venerant cum eo.
     EN: He brought him into the lodging, unloaded the camels, and provided straw and hay, and water to wash his feet and the feet of the men who had come with him.

24:33  LA: Et appositus est in conspectu ejus panis. Qui ait : Non comedam, donec loquar sermones meos. Respondit ei : Loquere.
     EN: Bread was set before him, but he said, “I will not eat until I have spoken my message.” He answered him, “Speak.”

24:34  LA: At ille : Servus, inquit, Abraham sum :
     EN: He said, “I am Abraham's servant.

24:35  LA: et Dominus benedixit domino meo valde, magnificatusque est : et dedit ei oves et boves, argentum et aurum, servos et ancillas, camelos et asinos.
     EN: The Lord has greatly blessed my master, and he has become great. He has given him sheep, oxen, silver, gold, male servants, maidservants, camels, and donkeys.

24:36  LA: Et peperit Sara uxor domini mei filium domino meo in senectute sua, deditque illi omnia quæ habuerat.
     EN: Sarah, my master's wife, bore my master a son in her old age, and he has given him everything he possessed.

24:37  LA: Et adjuravit me dominus meus, dicens : Non accipies uxorem filio meo de filiabus Chananæorum, in quorum terra habito :
     EN: My master made me swear, saying, ‘You shall not take a wife for my son from the daughters of the Canaanites, in whose land I live,

24:38  LA: sed ad domum patris mei perges, et de cognatione mea accipies uxorem filio meo.
     EN: but you shall go to my father's house and take a wife for my son from my kindred.’

24:39  LA: Ego vero respondi domino meo : Quid si noluerit venire mecum mulier ?
     EN: I answered my master, ‘What if the woman is unwilling to come with me?’

24:40  LA: Dominus, ait, in cujus conspectu ambulo, mittet angelum suum tecum, et diriget viam tuam : accipiesque uxorem filio meo de cognatione mea, et de domo patris mei.
     EN: He said, ‘The Lord, in whose presence I walk, will send His angel with you and direct your way. You shall take a wife for my son from my kindred and my father's house.

24:41  LA: Innocens eris a maledictione mea, cum veneris ad propinquos meos, et non dederint tibi.
     EN: You shall be free of my curse when you come to my relatives and they do not give her to you.’

24:42  LA: Veni ergo hodie ad fontem aquæ, et dixi : Domine Deus domini mei Abraham, si direxisti viam meam, in qua nunc ambulo,
     EN: “So today I came to the spring of water and said, ‘Lord, God of my master Abraham, if You have directed the way on which I now travel,

24:43  LA: ecce sto juxta fontem aquæ, et virgo, quæ egredietur ad hauriendam aquam, audierit a me : Da mihi pauxillum aquæ ad bibendum ex hydria tua :
     EN: see, I stand beside the spring of water. The virgin who comes out to draw water and hears me say, “Give me a little water to drink from your jar,”

24:44  LA: et dixerit mihi : Et tu bibe, et camelis tuis hauriam : ipsa est mulier, quam præparavit Dominus filio domini mei.
     EN: and who says to me, “Drink, and I will also draw for your camels”—she is the woman the Lord has prepared for my master's son.’

24:45  LA: Dumque hæc tacitus mecum volverem, apparuit Rebecca veniens cum hydria, quam portabat in scapula : descenditque ad fontem, et hausit aquam. Et aio ad eam : Da mihi paululum bibere.
     EN: While I was silently considering this within myself, Rebekah appeared, coming with a jar she carried upon her shoulder. She went down to the spring and drew water, and I said to her, ‘Give me a little to drink.’

24:46  LA: Quæ festinans deposuit hydriam de humero, et dixit mihi : Et tu bibe, et camelis tuis tribuam potum. Bibi, et adaquavit camelos.
     EN: She hurried to lower the jar from her shoulder and said to me, ‘Drink, and I will also give your camels water.’ I drank, and she watered the camels.

24:47  LA: Interrogavique eam, et dixi : Cujus es filia ? Quæ respondit : Filia Bathuelis sum, filii Nachor, quem peperit ei Melcha. Suspendi itaque inaures ad ornandam faciem ejus, et armillas posui in manibus ejus.
     EN: I asked her, ‘Whose daughter are you?’ She answered, ‘I am the daughter of Bethuel, Nahor's son, whom Milcah bore him.’ So I hung the earrings to adorn her face and put the bracelets on her hands.

24:48  LA: Pronusque adoravi Dominum, benedicens Domino Deo domini mei Abraham, qui perduxit me recto itinere, ut sumerem filiam fratris domini mei filio ejus.
     EN: I bowed down and worshiped the Lord, blessing the Lord, the God of my master Abraham, who led me by a straight way to take the daughter of my master's brother for his son.

24:49  LA: Quam ob rem si facitis misericordiam et veritatem cum domino meo, indicate mihi : sin autem aliud placet, et hoc dicite mihi, ut vadam ad dextram, sive ad sinistram.
     EN: Therefore, if you will show kindness and faithfulness to my master, tell me. But if something else pleases you, tell me that too, so that I may turn to the right or the left.”

24:50  LA: Responderuntque Laban et Bathuel : A Domino egressus est sermo : non possumus extra placitum ejus quidquam aliud loqui tecum.
     EN: Laban and Bethuel answered, “The matter has come from the Lord. We cannot speak anything else to you beyond His pleasure.

24:51  LA: En Rebecca coram te est, tolle eam, et proficiscere, et sit uxor filii domini tui, sicut locutus est Dominus.
     EN: Here is Rebekah before you. Take her and go, and let her be the wife of your master's son, as the Lord has spoken.”

24:52  LA: Quod cum audisset puer Abraham, procidens adoravit in terram Dominum.
     EN: When Abraham's servant heard this, he fell to the ground and worshiped the Lord.

24:53  LA: Prolatisque vasis argenteis, et aureis, ac vestibus, dedit ea Rebeccæ pro munere : fratribus quoque ejus et matri dona obtulit.
     EN: He brought out silver and gold vessels and garments, and gave them to Rebekah as gifts. He also offered gifts to her brothers and mother.

24:54  LA: Inito convivio, vescentes pariter et bibentes manserunt ibi. Surgens autem mane, locutus est puer : Dimitte me, ut vadam ad dominum meum.
     EN: They began a feast, eating and drinking together, and stayed there. In the morning the servant rose and said, “Send me away, that I may go to my master.”

24:55  LA: Responderuntque fratres ejus et mater : Maneat puella saltem decem dies apud nos, et postea proficiscetur.
     EN: Her brothers and mother answered, “Let the young woman remain with us at least ten days; afterward she shall go.”

24:56  LA: Nolite, ait, me retinere, quia Dominus direxit viam meam : dimittite me ut pergam ad dominum meum.
     EN: “Do not detain me,” he said, “for the Lord has directed my way. Send me away so that I may go to my master.”

24:57  LA: Et dixerunt : Vocemus puellam, et quæramus ipsius voluntatem.
     EN: They said, “Let us call the young woman and ask her own wishes.”

24:58  LA: Cumque vocata venisset, sciscitati sunt : Vis ire cum homine isto ? Quæ ait : Vadam.
     EN: When she had been called and came, they asked, “Will you go with this man?” She said, “I will go.”

24:59  LA: Dimiserunt ergo eam, et nutricem illius, servumque Abraham, et comites ejus,
     EN: So they sent her away with her nurse, Abraham's servant, and his companions,

24:60  LA: imprecantes prospera sorori suæ, atque dicentes : Soror nostra es, crescas in mille millia, et possideat semen tuum portas inimicorum suorum.
     EN: wishing their sister prosperity and saying, “You are our sister; may you increase to thousands of thousands, and may your offspring possess the gates of their enemies.”

24:61  LA: Igitur Rebecca et puellæ illius, ascensis camelis, secutæ sunt virum : qui festinus revertebatur ad dominum suum.
     EN: Rebekah and her maidens mounted the camels and followed the man, who hurried back to his master.

24:62  LA: Eo autem tempore deambulabat Isaac per viam quæ ducit ad puteum, cujus nomen est Viventis et videntis : habitabat enim in terra australi :
     EN: At that time Isaac was walking along the road leading to the well called The Living One Who Sees, for he lived in the southern land.

24:63  LA: et egressus fuerat ad meditandum in agro, inclinata jam die : cumque elevasset oculos, vidit camelos venientes procul.
     EN: He had gone out to meditate in the field as the day declined. Raising his eyes, he saw camels approaching in the distance.

24:64  LA: Rebecca quoque, conspecto Isaac, descendit de camelo,
     EN: Rebekah also saw Isaac and dismounted from the camel.

24:65  LA: et ait ad puerum : Quis est ille homo qui venit per agrum in occursum nobis ? Dixitque ei : Ipse est dominus meus. At illa tollens cito pallium, operuit se.
     EN: She said to the servant, “Who is that man coming through the field to meet us?” He said, “He is my master.” She quickly took her cloak and covered herself.

24:66  LA: Servus autem cuncta, quæ gesserat, narravit Isaac.
     EN: The servant told Isaac everything he had done.

24:67  LA: Qui introduxit eam in tabernaculum Saræ matris suæ, et accepit eam uxorem : et in tantum dilexit eam, ut dolorem, qui ex morte matris ejus acciderat, temperaret.
     EN: Isaac brought her into the tent of his mother Sarah and took her as his wife. He loved her so greatly that he found relief from the grief caused by his mother's death.

GENESIS 25

25:1  LA: Abraham vero aliam duxit uxorem nomine Ceturam :
     EN: Abraham took another wife, named Keturah.

25:2  LA: quæ peperit ei Zamran et Jecsan, et Madan, et Madian, et Jesboc, et Sue.
     EN: She bore him Zimran, Jokshan, Medan, Midian, Ishbak, and Shuah.
